The package uses the existing Clstr Vizag documentation and public evidence review. The pricing anchors are internal pilot hypotheses and must be replaced by observed proposal, cash-collection, margin and renewal data. Local student totals, event demand and sponsor willingness to pay remain unproven until Clstr measures them.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:3:**Scope:** The Clstr Vizag student community at `vizag.clstr.in`.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:5:> **Your college gets you a degree. Clstr gets you a city.**
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:9:Clstr Vizag is not broken because the ambition is too large. It is exposed because the operating proof is too small for the ambition. The turnaround is not to add more event ideas, recruit more ambassadors or make larger claims. The turnaround is to prove one repeatable loop:
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:11:> **A student discovers Clstr → joins and finds a relevant person or space → participates in a real experience → forms a cross-college connection → returns to `clstr.in` between events → invites another student.**
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:13:Only after that loop is measurable should Clstr sell larger sponsorships, build expensive features, promise founder income or expand beyond Vizag.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:15:The community is the product. `clstr.in` is the supporting identity, discovery, continuity and measurement layer. This distinction is mandatory in every public, partner and recruitment document.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:23:| P0 | `clstr.in` may be only registration | The app is not a moat if students use it once and return to other channels. | Make discovery, Circle/Guild participation, connections and post-event follow-up essential. |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:24:| P0 | Sponsor revenue is assumed | Interest, logos and verbal promises are not revenue. | Count only contracted and collected cash; pursue one narrow paid pilot and renewal. |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:25:| P1 | Campus Ambassador count hides inactivity | Appointed ambassadors can create the appearance of distribution without active nodes. | Measure verified participation, repeat activity, reporting, conduct and backup coverage. |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:28:| P1 | Event economics are unclear | A large crowd can still produce a loss after venue, production, safety, refunds and founder time. | Approve every event with a budget and close it with contribution-margin actuals. |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:29:| P1 | Trust, safety and privacy controls are incomplete | Student identity, photos, minors, events and partner access create material risk. | Implement consent, moderation, reporting, safeguarding and role-based access before scale. |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:38:| The unqualified claim that Clstr is the only or first student community in Vizag | Existing campus ecosystems and city communities are real alternatives. Clstr should claim the narrower category of cross-college student social discovery, not deny the ecosystem. [1] [2] [3] |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:39:| Publishing unverified student-population or institution totals as facts | A large denominator does not prove demand. Use verified institution data or label figures as internal hypotheses. |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:40:| Recruiting ambassadors primarily to forward posters or grow WhatsApp groups | It creates vanity distribution, spam and weak retention. A node must produce meaningful participation and feedback. |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:41:| Announcing large events before venue, permission, safety, capacity, budget and fallback funding are approved | This converts an aspiration into financial and reputational exposure. |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:42:| Building broad app features before one useful loop works | Empty profiles, inactive groups and unused features make `clstr.in` look like infrastructure without a community. |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:43:| Selling “access to students,” contact lists or guaranteed leads | It damages trust and creates privacy, compliance and partner-expectation risk. |
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:45:| Promising founder salary, equity, revenue share or future employment without written funded terms | This creates internal distrust and personal financial risk. |

./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:61:Do not build a social feed, marketplace, gamification system, job platform, complex recommendation engine or national feature set until students repeatedly use the core journey.

./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:114:### Security, privacy and safety
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:116:**Fix:** Treat trust and safety as product infrastructure, not paperwork.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:121:- define age eligibility and a separate safeguarding process for minors;
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:122:- collect only necessary data;
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:123:- separate community consent from sponsor or opportunity consent;
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:126:- obtain photo/video consent for public use;
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:129:- obtain qualified Indian legal/privacy review before significant data sharing or minor participation at scale. [4]
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:131:**Red line:** No sponsor receives a general student database or unrestricted contact access.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:137:**Action:** Create one-page checklists for event approval, venue and permission verification, budget approval, safety, check-in, incident response, post-event follow-up, sponsor delivery and financial close. Every recurring task has a primary owner and backup.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:145:**Action:** Give each Campus Ambassador or Core Team member a written scope, weekly time expectation, named manager, first deliverable, feedback schedule, contribution record and exit route. The published Campus Ambassador sequence remains:
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:147:> **M01 Certificate → M02 Recommendation Letter → M03 Merch → M04 Tee/VIP Passes/Recognition.**
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:149:The rewards are earned through documented work and are not salary. No certificate should be issued merely for joining, and no recommendation should contain claims the record cannot support.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:153:Review the scorecard every month. Do not replace these measures with follower count, QR scans or the number of appointed ambassadors.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:160:| `clstr.in` habit | Discovery, Circle/Guild, connection and follow-up actions between events | Tests whether the supporting layer creates continuity. |

./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:184:**Exit gate:** Stable operations, controlled safety process, useful between-event activity, repeat participants and no single-person dependency for routine delivery.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:194:Add only the next feature or format that improves retention or partner value. Build node-level reporting, contributor progression, sponsor case studies and a repeatable proposal template. Test whether the team can deliver two consecutive cycles without founder rescue.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:200:Choose one of three decisions: deepen Vizag, change the model, or prepare a tightly controlled second-city experiment. A second city is allowed only with a local operator, budget, safety controls, documented playbook, minimum proof threshold and kill threshold.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:202:**Exit gate for expansion:** Six months of stable retention, repeatable partner revenue, controlled incidents, protected reserve and routine operations that continue without the founder.
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:204:## 7. Revenue and founder-income gates
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:208:- recurring or repeatable paid revenue;
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:213:- meaningful `clstr.in` usage between events;
./Clstr_Vizag_Operator_Turnaround_and_Scaling_Plan.md:223:Clstr Vizag wins if it becomes the trusted place where students meet beyond their own college and keep participating. `clstr.in` becomes strategically valuable only when it carries identity, discovery, connections and follow-up between real experiences. Sponsorship becomes a business only when partners pay for clearly defined outcomes and renew.
